     3	// Best-effort extraction of baton events from raw harness hook payloads.
     4	// Total: any input value yields an Event[] — garbage degrades to note events,
     5	// never a throw, so a checkpoint can never break the host harness.

     7	/** @typedef {{host: string, pid: number, startTime: number | null}} SessionFacts */
     8	
     9	/** @type {Record<string, string>} */
    10	const FILE_TOOL_OPS = { Write: 'write', Edit: 'edit' };
    11	
    12	/** @returns {SessionFacts} */
    13	function processFacts() {
    14	  return { host: hostname(), pid: process.pid, startTime: Math.round(performance.timeOrigin) };
    15	}
    16	
    17	/**
    18	 * A process-scoped hint for payloads lacking a stable session id. Deterministic
    19	 * over the identity facts: one process => one hint; unstable hints never
    20	 * trigger foreign-session rejection on their own (enforced at the command).
    21	 * @param {SessionFacts | undefined} session
    22	 */
    23	function generatedHint(session) {
    24	  const f = session ?? processFacts();
    25	  return ['unstable', f.host, f.pid, f.startTime].join(':');
    26	}
    27	
    28	/**
    29	 * @param {any} raw
    30	 * @param {SessionFacts | undefined} session
    31	 * @returns {{sessionHint: string, unstable: boolean}}
    32	 */
    33	function deriveHint(raw, session) {
    34	  // Cursor identifies the session as conversation_id (audit finding 26): with
    35	  // only session_id read, every cursor hook fire fell back to a per-process
    36	  // unstable hint — a warning on every invocation and ownership adoption that
    37	  // never engaged.
    38	  const id = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? (raw.session_id ?? raw.conversation_id) : undefined;
    39	  if (typeof id === 'string' && id.length > 0) return { sessionHint: id, unstable: false };
    40	  return { sessionHint: generatedHint(session), unstable: true };
    41	}
    42	
    43	/**
    44	 * Normalize a parsed hook payload into baton/event@1 events. Adapters that
    45	 * already speak baton/event@1 pass through verbatim; raw per-harness payloads
    46	 * get best-effort extraction; unknowns degrade to note events. Never throws.
    47	 * @param {any} raw parsed JS value (the caller owns JSON.parse)
    48	 * @param {string} platform 'claude-code' | 'codex' | 'cursor'
    49	 * @param {SessionFacts} [session] identity facts for generated hints; defaults to this process
    50	 * @param {string} [explicitEvent] the hook event name passed on the command line
    51	 *   (`--trigger`). Authoritative when present — the hooks manifest already keys
    52	 *   each command by event, so this needs no per-harness payload parsing. Cursor's
    53	 *   stop payload carries neither `hook_event_name` nor `event`, so without this
    54	 *   its checkpoint degraded to trigger 'unknown' and never satisfied the canary.
    55	 * @returns {any[]}
    56	 */
    57	export function normalizeHookPayload(raw, platform, session, explicitEvent) {
    58	  // Passthrough tier: the adapter formed the events; return them untouched.
    59	  if (raw && typeof raw === 'object' && raw.schema === 'baton/event@1') {
    60	    if (Array.isArray(raw.events)) return raw.events;
    61	    return [raw];
    62	  }
    63	
    64	  const { sessionHint, unstable } = deriveHint(raw, session);
    65	  /** @param {string} type @param {Record<string, any>} payload */
    66	  const event = (type, payload) => ({ type, payload, source: platform, sessionHint, unstable });
    67	  /** @param {string} trigger */
    68	  const note = (trigger) => event('note', { trigger, text: 'hook ' + trigger + ' observed on ' + platform });
    69	
    70	  const eventName =
    71	    (typeof explicitEvent === 'string' && explicitEvent.length > 0 && explicitEvent) ||
    72	    (raw && typeof raw === 'object'
    73	      ? (typeof raw.hook_event_name === 'string' && raw.hook_event_name) ||
    74	        (platform === 'cursor' && typeof raw.event === 'string' && raw.event) ||
    75	        null
    76	      : null);
    77	  if (!eventName) return [note('unknown')];
    78	
    79	  if (platform === 'claude-code') {
    80	    if (eventName === 'PostToolUse') {
    81	      const op = FILE_TOOL_OPS[raw.tool_name];
    82	      const path = raw.tool_input && typeof raw.tool_input === 'object' ? raw.tool_input.file_path : undefined;
    83	      if (op && typeof path === 'string') return [event('file.touch', { path, op })];
    84	      return [note('PostToolUse')];
    85	    }
    86	    if (eventName === 'StopFailure') {
    87	      const errorType = raw.error && typeof raw.error === 'object' && typeof raw.error.type === 'string' ? raw.error.type : 'unknown';
    88	      return [
    89	        event('note', {
    90	          trigger: 'StopFailure',
    91	          text: 'turn ended on API error: ' + errorType,
    92	          errorType,
    93	          structured: { kind: 'stop-failure', errorType },
    94	        }),
    95	      ];
    96	    }
    97	    return [note(eventName)];
    98	  }
    99	
   100	  // Codex and Cursor (and any future platform string): every lifecycle event
   101	  // is a note carrying its trigger; the harness is the source.
   102	  return [note(eventName)];
   103	}

    24	/**
    25	 * Opt-in transcript-tail capture (gate-2 fix 10). Returns the bounded,
    26	 * redacted tail or null when the gate is closed: config off (the default),
    27	 * not a PreCompact payload, no usable transcript_path, or an unreadable /
    28	 * symlinked file. The path comes from an untrusted hook payload — it is read
    29	 * only under explicit opt-in, must lstat as a regular file, and its content
    30	 * passes the secret-redaction filter before storage.
    31	 * @param {string} root @param {any} raw parsed hook payload @param {any} io
    32	 * @returns {string | null}
    33	 */
    34	function captureTranscriptTail(root, raw, io) {
    35	  const cfg = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${root}/baton.config.json`);
    36	  if (!cfg.ok || cfg.value?.capture?.transcriptTail !== true) return null;
    37	
    38	  const eventName = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? (raw.hook_event_name ?? raw.event) : null;
    39	  if (typeof eventName !== 'string' || !/precompact/i.test(eventName)) return null;
    40	  const path = raw.transcript_path;
    41	  if (typeof path !== 'string' || path.length === 0) return null;
    42	
    43	  try {
    44	    if (!io.fs.lstatSync(path).isFile()) return null;
    45	    // Allowlist the transcript path (gate-2 iter-2 m1): the payload is
    46	    // untrusted, so its resolved realpath must sit under the repo root, the
    47	    // user's ~/.claude tree (where Claude Code actually writes transcripts),
    48	    // or a configured capture.transcriptDir — never an arbitrary readable file.
    49	    const fileReal = io.fs.realpathSync(path);
    50	    // On POSIX a backslash is a LEGAL filename character, not a separator, so
    51	    // normalizing '\\'→'/' in the containment check (isContained) could make an
    52	    // out-of-tree sibling like '/repo\\evil/f' appear contained (iter-4 I9).
    53	    // The transcript path is the one fully-untrusted path that reaches
    54	    // containment, so refuse a backslash-bearing realpath off Windows.
    55	    if (io.platform !== 'win32' && String(fileReal).includes('\\')) return null;
    56	    /** @type {string[]} */
    57	    const allowed = [root];
    58	    const home = io.env?.HOME;
    59	    if (typeof home === 'string' && home.length > 0) allowed.push(`${home}/.claude`);
    60	    const dir = cfg.value?.capture?.transcriptDir;
    61	    if (typeof dir === 'string' && dir.length > 0) allowed.push(dir);
    62	    const under = (/** @type {string} */ base) => {
    63	      let baseReal;
    64	      try {
    65	        baseReal = io.fs.realpathSync(base);
    66	      } catch {
    67	        baseReal = base;
    68	      }
    69	      // Compare on normalized forward-slash spellings (iter-3 F9): raw Windows
    70	      // backslash realpaths never satisfy a forward-slash containment, so
    71	      // opt-in capture would silently never fire.
    72	      return isContained(baseReal, fileReal);
    73	    };
    74	    if (!allowed.some(under)) return null;
    75	
    76	    const text = io.fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
    77	    const lines = text.split('\n').filter((/** @type {string} */ l) => l.trim() !== '');
    78	    let tail = lines.slice(-TAIL_MESSAGES).join('\n');
    79	    if (Buffer.byteLength(tail) > TAIL_BYTES) {
    80	      tail = Buffer.from(tail).subarray(-TAIL_BYTES).toString('utf8');
    81	    }
    82	    return redactSecrets(tail);
    83	  } catch {
    84	    return null; // unreadable transcript is never a checkpoint failure
    85	  }
    86	}

    88	/** Event-shaped: an object carrying a string `type`. */
    89	const isEventShaped = (/** @type {any} */ v) => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) && typeof v.type === 'string';
    90	
    91	/**
    92	 * Tolerant stdin parse (live /baton:handoff failure: a model sent one event
    93	 * per line and the whole narrative checkpoint silently failed). Accept the
    94	 * canonical single JSON value, a bare array of events, or NDJSON where every
    95	 * line is an event object or a {schema, events} wrapper. All-or-nothing: one
    96	 * non-event element fails the whole parse — never a partial apply.
    97	 * @param {string} text @returns {{ok: true, raw: any} | {ok: false, why?: string}}
    98	 */
    99	function parseStdin(text) {
   100	  let single;
   101	  let singleOk = false;
   102	  try {
   103	    single = JSON.parse(text);
   104	    singleOk = true;
   105	  } catch {
   106	    singleOk = false;
   107	  }
   108	  if (singleOk) {
   109	    if (Array.isArray(single)) {
   110	      return single.every(isEventShaped) ? { ok: true, raw: { schema: 'baton/event@1', events: single } } : { ok: false };
   111	    }
   112	    // A closest-miss of the documented wrapper — an events array whose schema
   113	    // key is missing or wrong — must ERROR, never silently degrade to a junk
   114	    // note with ok:true (the model cannot self-correct from a success
   115	    // envelope while its narrative was discarded).
   116	    if (single !== null && typeof single === 'object' && Array.isArray(single.events) && single.schema !== 'baton/event@1') {
   117	      return { ok: false, why: `stdin carries an events array but schema is ${single.schema === undefined ? 'missing' : JSON.stringify(single.schema)} — use {"schema":"baton/event@1","events":[…]}` };
   118	    }
   119	    // A single bare event object (string `type`, none of the raw-hook payload
   120	    // markers) is one of the two most likely readings of "pipe events on
   121	    // stdin" — accept it as that event instead of degrading it to a junk note.
   122	    if (
   123	      isEventShaped(single) &&
   124	      single.schema === undefined &&
   125	      !('hook_event_name' in single) &&
   126	      !('session_id' in single) &&
   127	      !('event' in single)
   128	    ) {
   129	      return { ok: true, raw: { schema: 'baton/event@1', events: [single] } };
   130	    }
   131	    return { ok: true, raw: single };
   132	  }
   133	  const lines = text.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter((l) => l !== '');
   134	  if (lines.length === 0) return { ok: false };
   135	  /** @type {any[]} */
   136	  const events = [];
   137	  for (const line of lines) {
   138	    let v;
   139	    try {
   140	      v = JSON.parse(line);
   141	    } catch {
   142	      return { ok: false };
   143	    }
   144	    if (v && typeof v === 'object' && v.schema === 'baton/event@1' && Array.isArray(v.events)) {
   145	      if (!v.events.every(isEventShaped)) return { ok: false };
   146	      events.push(...v.events);
   147	    } else if (isEventShaped(v)) {
   148	      events.push(v);
   149	    } else {
   150	      return { ok: false };
   151	    }
   152	  }
   153	  return { ok: true, raw: { schema: 'baton/event@1', events } };
   154	}
   155	
   156	/**
   157	 * `baton checkpoint` — mechanical checkpoint from a hook payload on stdin.
   158	 * Hook-safety rule: exit 0 on every soft failure so a checkpoint can never
   159	 * break the host harness; `--strict` opts into hard failures (CI).
   160	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
   161	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   162	 */
   163	export async function cmdCheckpoint(args, io) {
   164	  if (isSupervisedChild(io)) return 0;
   165	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { platform: 'string', model: 'string', trigger: 'string', debounce: 'string', strict: 'boolean', 'take-over': 'boolean' });
   166	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'checkpoint', parsed.error);
   167	  const flags = parsed.flags;
   168	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
   169	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;
   170	  if (!platform) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--platform <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
   171	  const pErr = platformError(platform);
   172	  if (pErr) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', pErr);
   173	
   174	  try {
   175	    return await run(flags, platform, io);
   176	  } catch (err) {
   177	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
   178	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'checkpoint-failed', msg } });
   179	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${msg}\n`);
   180	    return strict ? 1 : 0;
   181	  }
   182	}
   183	
   184	/**
   185	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
   186	 * @param {string} platform
   187	 * @param {any} io
   188	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   189	 */
   190	async function run(flags, platform, io) {
   191	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
   192	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
   193	  const paths = bundlePaths(root);
   194	  // The --json contract (gate-2 fix 11): exactly one envelope on stdout for
   195	  // every exit path — the exit code stays governed by hook-safety.
   196	  const finish = (/** @type {any} */ env, /** @type {number} */ code) => {
   197	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, env);
   198	    return code;
   199	  };
   200	
   201	  const parsed = parseStdin(typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '');
   202	  if (!parsed.ok) {
   203	    const why = parsed.why ?? 'send one JSON value ({"schema":"baton/event@1","events":[…]}), a JSON array of events, or NDJSON event lines';
   204	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: stdin could not be parsed — ${why}\n`);
   205	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-stdin', msg: why } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
   206	  }
   207	  const raw = parsed.raw;
   208	
   209	  const session = { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime };
   210	  // The hook command declares which event it is (--trigger), so event identity
   211	  // never depends on parsing each harness's payload shape (Cursor's stop payload
   212	  // names the event nowhere normalize could find it).
   213	  const explicitEvent = typeof flags.trigger === 'string' ? flags.trigger : undefined;
   214	  const events = normalizeHookPayload(raw, platform, session, explicitEvent);
   215	  if (events.length === 0) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false } }, 0);
   216	
   217	  // Opt-in transcript tail (gate-2 fix 10): rides the journal as its own
   218	  // IMPORTANT event so the snapshot rewrite below persists it, and purge can
   219	  // strip it from every retained copy by its property name.
   220	  const tail = captureTranscriptTail(root, raw, io);
   221	  if (tail !== null) {
   222	    events.push({
   223	      type: 'transcript.set',
   224	      payload: { transcript: { capturedAt: io.now(), tail } },
   225	      source: platform,
   226	      sessionHint: events[0]?.sessionHint,
   227	      unstable: events[0]?.unstable ?? true,
   228	    });
   229	  }
   230	
   231	  const { bundle, warnings, unsafe } = loadBundle(root, io);
   232	  for (const w of warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${w}\n`);
   233	
   234	  // An unsafe managed tree (symlinked .handoff — gate-2 fix 6) is NOT "no
   235	  // bundle yet": seeding here would write through the link. Refuse everything;
   236	  // hook-safety keeps the refusal soft outside --strict.
   237	  if (unsafe === true) {
   238	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: managed tree failed the symlink/realpath jail — nothing written\n');
   239	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'unsafe-tree', msg: warnings[0] ?? 'managed tree failed the jail check' } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
   240	  }
   241	
   242	  // Opt-in debounce (gate-2 major 14; Cursor afterFileEdit fires per edit):
   243	  // at most one real checkpoint per window, tracked by a per-platform stamp.
   244	  // The read-and-set runs UNDER the repo lock (gate-2 iter-2 M5) so two
   245	  // simultaneous afterFileEdit hooks can't both observe no stamp and both
   246	  // checkpoint — exactly one wins the window.
   247	  if (flags.debounce !== undefined) {
   248	    const seconds = Number(flags.debounce);
   249	    if (!Number.isFinite(seconds) || seconds <= 0) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--debounce <seconds> must be a positive number');
   250	    const stampPath = `${paths.logDir}/debounce-${platform}.json`;
   251	    const proceed = withLock(root, io, () => {
   252	      const stamp = safeReadJson(io.fs, stampPath);
   253	      if (stamp.ok && typeof stamp.value?.at === 'string' && Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(stamp.value.at) < seconds * 1000) {
   254	        return false;
   255	      }
   256	      ensureDir(io.fs, paths.logDir);
   257	      atomicWriteJson(io.fs, stampPath, { at: io.now() });
   258	      return true;
   259	    });
   260	    if (!proceed) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false, debounced: true } }, 0);
   261	  }
   262	
   263	  let active = bundle;
   264	  let mustRewrite = false;
   265	
   266	  // Semantic isolation (plan §Concurrency): a stable foreign session or a
   267	  // mismatched origin platform must not merge into the active bundle. Checked
   268	  // before ANY write so a rejection leaves the whole tree untouched.
   269	  const originHint = active?.origin?.sessionHint;
   270	  const hasStableOwner = active !== null && typeof originHint === 'string' && originHint.length > 0 && active.origin.unstable !== true;
   271	  const stableIncoming = events.find(
   272	    (/** @type {any} */ e) => typeof e.sessionHint === 'string' && e.sessionHint.length > 0 && e.unstable === false,
   273	  );
   274	  const unstableIncoming = events.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => e.unstable === true);
   275	
   276	  if (hasStableOwner) {
   277	    // Foreign = a different stable session, OR any platform mismatch — the
   278	    // platform is provable even when the events carry no session hint (audit
   279	    // finding: a hint-less /baton:handoff narrative from claude-code silently
   280	    // merged into — then sealed — a codex-owned bundle, corrupting the origin
   281	    // the receive-side role avoidance keys on).
   282	    const incomingLabel = stableIncoming ? stableIncoming.sessionHint : '(no session hint)';
   283	    const foreign = stableIncoming
   284	      ? stableIncoming.sessionHint !== originHint || platform !== active.origin.platform
   285	      : platform !== active.origin.platform;
   286	    if (foreign && flags['take-over'] !== true) {
   287	      io.stderr.write(
   288	        `baton checkpoint: event from a foreign session/origin (${platform}/${incomingLabel} vs active ${active.origin.platform}/${originHint}) — rejected to keep sessions isolated; rerun with --take-over to archive the active bundle and start fresh\n`,
   289	      );
   290	      return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'foreign-session', msg: 'event from a foreign session/origin rejected; rerun with --take-over' } }, 0);
   291	    }
   292	    if (foreign) {
   293	      rotateJournal(root, 'takeover', io);
   294	      const fresh = emptyBundle(
   295	        { platform, model: typeof flags.model === 'string' ? flags.model : 'unknown', goal: active.task?.goal ?? 'unknown' },
   296	        io.now(),
   297	      );
   298	      if (stableIncoming) fresh.origin.sessionHint = stableIncoming.sessionHint;
   299	      writeSnapshot(root, fresh, io);
   300	      io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: took over — prior bundle archived to history/, fresh bundle owned by ${platform}/${incomingLabel}\n`);
   301	      active = fresh;
   302	      mustRewrite = true;
   303	    }
   304	  }
   305	
   306	  if (unstableIncoming) {
   307	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: event carries an unstable (generated) session hint — ownership unverifiable; applied with this warning only\n');
   308	  }
   309	
   310	  if (active === null) {
   311	    active = emptyBundle(
   312	      { platform, model: typeof flags.model === 'string' ? flags.model : 'unknown', goal: 'unknown' },
   313	      io.now(),
   314	    );
   315	    writeSnapshot(root, active, io);
   316	    // Self-applying seed event (gate-2 iter-2 B6): a journal-only rebuild
   317	    // (snapshot + .bak both lost) must restore the bundle's identity, not the
   318	    // "unknown" placeholder resolveBase seeds. bundle.seed carries bundleId,
   319	    // origin, task, and generation and is applied on replay.
   320	    appendJournal(
   321	      root,
   322	      {
   323	        ts: io.now(),
   324	        type: 'bundle.seed',
   325	        payload: {
   326	          bundleId: active.bundleId,
   327	          generation: active.generation,
   328	          createdAt: active.createdAt,
   329	          origin: active.origin,
   330	          task: { goal: active.task.goal },
   331	          text: `[seed] auto-seeded a new bundle on ${platform} (model ${active.origin.model})`,
   332	        },
   333	        writerId: [platform, io.pid, 'seed'].join('-'),
   334	        dedupeKey: dedupeKey({ ts: io.now(), type: 'bundle.seed', bundleId: active.bundleId }),
   335	      },
   336	      io,
   337	    );
   338	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: no bundle found — auto-seeded a new one at .handoff/bundle.json\n');
   339	    mustRewrite = true;
   340	  }
   341	
   342	  // Ownership adoption (gate-2 fix 2): a bundle whose owner is unverified — a
   343	  // fresh seed (null hint) or a receive that defaulted to an unstable hint —
   344	  // adopts the first STABLE same-platform session as its owner, so semantic
   345	  // isolation engages from then on instead of never.
   346	  const ownerUnverified = active.origin.sessionHint === null || active.origin.unstable === true;
   347	  if (ownerUnverified && stableIncoming && active.origin.platform === platform) {
   348	    active = { ...active, origin: { ...active.origin, sessionHint: stableIncoming.sessionHint, unstable: false } };
   349	    writeSnapshot(root, active, io);
   350	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: adopted ${platform}/${stableIncoming.sessionHint} as the bundle owner (first verified session)\n`);
   351	    mustRewrite = true;
   352	  }
   353	
   354	  let lastSeq = 0;
   355	  for (const ev of events) {
   356	    const ts = typeof ev.ts === 'string' ? ev.ts : io.now();
   357	    const entry = {
   358	      ...ev,
   359	      ts,
   360	      dedupeKey:
   361	        typeof ev.dedupeKey === 'string' && ev.dedupeKey.length > 0
   362	          ? ev.dedupeKey
   363	          : dedupeKey({ ts, type: ev.type, payload: ev.payload, source: ev.source, sessionHint: ev.sessionHint }),
   364	      writerId: [platform, io.pid, ev.sessionHint ?? 'local'].join('-'),
   365	    };
   366	    lastSeq = appendJournal(root, entry, io);
   367	  }
   368	
   369	  const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, paths.snapshot);
   370	  const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   371	  const snapUpdated = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.updatedAt === 'string' ? Date.parse(snap.value.updatedAt) : 0;
   372	  const important = events.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => !ROUTINE_TYPES.has(e.type));
   373	  const elapsed = Date.parse(io.now()) - snapUpdated > THROTTLE_WINDOW_MS;
   374	  const accreted = lastSeq - snapSeq;
   375	
   376	  let rewritten = false;
   377	  if (mustRewrite || important || elapsed || accreted >= THROTTLE_EVENT_COUNT) {
   378	    // Bounded git refresh on EVERY snapshot rewrite (gate-2 iter-2 M1): a
   379	    // mechanical checkpoint carries branch/HEAD/dirty. Capture git BEFORE the
   380	    // lock — it is async and must not straddle the lock — then do ONE atomic
   381	    // read-modify-write UNDER the lock (iter-5 A1): reload the bundle inside the
   382	    // lock, apply git, and write with the fencing token. The old code loaded
   383	    // outside the lock, awaited git (yielding the event loop), then wrote the
   384	    // stale bundle, so a concurrent --take-over during the await was clobbered
   385	    // (origin rolled back, the new owner's event lost). Reloading inside the
   386	    // lock guarantees the write is built from current state.
   387	    const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   388	    rewritten = withLock(root, io, (token) => {
   389	      const merged = loadBundle(root, io).bundle;
   390	      if (!merged) return false;
   391	      if (git !== null) {
   392	        merged.git = git;
   393	      } else {
   394	        // git refresh failed/timed out (iter-4 I2/F5): NEVER retain the prior
   395	        // snapshot — stale HEAD/dirty presented as current corrupts the
   396	        // receive-side evidence audit. Clear to explicit unavailable (null) and
   397	        // warn; a repo that was never git (already null) warns nothing.
   398	        if (merged.git !== null) {
   399	          io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: git refresh failed — clearing stale git state (marked unavailable; receive re-derives it live)\n');
   400	        }
   401	        merged.git = null;
   402	      }
   403	      writeSnapshotIn(root, merged, io, token);
   404	      return true;
   405	    });
   406	  }
   407	  return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: events.length, rewritten } }, 0);
   408	}

     9	// RED — `baton checkpoint --session <hint>` (subtask l1-checkpoint-session).
    10	//
    11	// Source of truth: docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-tasks.md subtask 2
    12	//   (`checkpoint --session <hint>` flag: supervisor-owned stable session
    13	//   identity) and docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md
    14	//   §"Root + ownership discipline".
    15	//
    16	// The loop supervisor must checkpoint under its OWN stable session identity
    17	// (`--session loop-<runId>`) regardless of what the piped hook payload carries,
    18	// so its checkpoints land in ITS bundle and a concurrent child's payload
    19	// session id can never masquerade as, or collide with, the supervisor owner.
    20	//
    21	// CONTRACT (pinned here):
    22	//   S1. STRICT SPEC. `--session <hint>` is a KNOWN string flag: `--session abc`
    23	//       parses and composes with the existing flags; a stray positional or an
    24	//       unknown flag alongside it still exits 2 (unchanged strict parsing).
    25	//   S2. NO SILENT FALLBACK. `--session ''` (empty value) is a usage error
    26	//       (exit 2) whose message NAMES the flag and states it must be non-empty —
    27	//       a supervisor bug that passes an empty hint must NOT silently fall back
    28	//       to payload-derived identity. (Distinct from today's "unknown flag":
    29	//       the message must mention "empty", never "unknown flag".)
    30	//   S3. IDENTITY OVERRIDE. With `--session sup-1`, every checkpoint event's

    32	//       the piped payload carries a DIFFERENT stable session_id. Asserted via
    33	//       persisted state: origin.sessionHint and the journal writerId.
    34	//   S4. OWNERSHIP SEMANTICS UNCHANGED, keyed on the effective (overridden) hint:

    37	//       (b) first-party — a second --session checkpoint with the same hint
    38	//           lands (no foreign rejection);
    39	//       (c) foreign — a --session X checkpoint into a bundle owned by stable

    41	//           (exit 0, --take-over named, memfs byte-identical); --take-over
    42	//           still supersedes (fresh bundle owned by X);

    44	//   S5. GUARD PRECEDENCE. BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD set + --session → still the

    46	//

    13	export function bundlePaths(root) {

    16	    dir,
    17	    snapshot: `${dir}/bundle.json`,

    27	/** @param {string} iso */
    28	const tsForFile = (iso) => iso.replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
    29	
    30	/** @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p */
    31	const markerPath = (p) => `${p.dir}/rotation.marker.json`;
    32	
    33	/**
    34	 * Reconcile a leftover rotation marker (crash recovery, part of load). The
    35	 * mutations run under the repo lock, acquired WITHOUT waiting (gate-2 minor
    36	 * 16): a held lock means someone is mid-operation — reconciliation defers to
    37	 * the next unlocked load instead of writing behind their back.
    38	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings @param {string} root
    39	 */
    40	function reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root) {
    41	  const mp = markerPath(p);
    42	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return;
    43	  try {
    44	    withLock(root, { ...io, lockRetry: { attempts: 0, delayMs: 0 } }, () => {
    45	      if (!io.fs.existsSync(mp)) return; // a competitor reconciled while we acquired
    46	      const marker = safeReadJson(io.fs, mp);
    47	      if (!marker.ok) {
    48	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    49	        warnings.push('rotation marker was unparseable — rolled the rotation back (marker cleared, journal preserved)');
    50	        return;
    51	      }
    52	      const stem = `${tsForFile(marker.value.startedAt)}.${marker.value.kind}`;
    53	      const freeze = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`;
    54	      const rotated = `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`;
    55	
    56	      if (io.fs.existsSync(freeze)) {
    57	        // Roll forward: finish whichever step the crash interrupted.
    58	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal) && !io.fs.existsSync(rotated)) {
    59	          io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, rotated);
    60	        }
    61	        if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
    62	          atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
    63	        }
    64	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    65	        warnings.push(`completed an interrupted rotation (roll-forward of ${stem}); marker cleared`);
    66	      } else {
    67	        io.fs.unlinkSync(mp);
    68	        warnings.push(`aborted an incomplete rotation (roll-back; ${stem} freeze never landed); marker cleared, journal preserved`);
    69	      }
    70	    });
    71	  } catch (err) {
    72	    if (err instanceof LockHeldError) {
    73	      warnings.push('a rotation marker is present but the repo lock is held — reconciliation deferred to the next unlocked load');
    74	      return;
    75	    }
    76	    throw err;
    77	  }
    78	}
    79	
    80	/**
    81	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof bundlePaths>} p @param {string[]} warnings
    82	 * @returns {any | null} a usable base bundle, or null when nothing exists
    83	 */
    84	function resolveBase(io, p, warnings) {
    85	  const snapshotExists = io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot);
    86	  if (snapshotExists) {
    87	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
    88	    if (snap.ok && validateBundle(snap.value).ok) return snap.value;
    89	    warnings.push(
    90	      snap.ok
    91	        ? 'snapshot bundle.json failed schema validation — attempting recovery'
    92	        : `snapshot bundle.json is unreadable (${snap.error}) — attempting recovery`,
    93	    );
    94	    const bak = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.bak);
    95	    if (bak.ok && validateBundle(bak.value).ok) {
    96	      warnings.push('recovered from backup bundle.json.bak; replaying journal past its journalSeq');
    97	      return bak.value;
    98	    }
    99	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.bak)) warnings.push('backup bundle.json.bak is also unusable');
   100	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   101	      warnings.push('rebuilding the bundle by replaying the whole journal over a fresh seed (recovery of last resort)');
   102	      return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
   103	    }
   104	    return null;
   105	  }
   106	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   107	    warnings.push('no snapshot present — rebuilding the bundle from the journal');
   108	    return emptyBundle({ platform: 'unknown', model: 'unknown', goal: 'unknown (rebuilt from journal)' }, io.now());
   109	  }
   110	  return null;
   111	}
   112	
   113	/**
   114	 * Load the active bundle with full crash recovery. Read path — not lock-gated;
   115	 * rotation-marker reconciliation is part of load. A managed tree that fails
   116	 * the symlink/realpath jail (gate-2 fix 6) is refused outright: bundle null,
   117	 * `unsafe: true`, and the problem in warnings — callers must not treat this as
   118	 * "no bundle yet" and seed through the link.
   119	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   120	 * @returns {{bundle: any | null, warnings: string[], unsafe?: boolean}}
   121	 */
   122	export function loadBundle(root, io) {
   123	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   124	  /** @type {string[]} */
   125	  const warnings = [];
   126	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.dir)) return { bundle: null, warnings };
   127	
   128	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   129	  if (!safe.ok) return { bundle: null, warnings: [safe.problem], unsafe: true };
   130	
   131	  reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root);
   132	
   133	  // An interrupted purge is visible on every read path (gate-2 minor 16): the
   134	  // tree may still hold transcript copies until purge-transcript finishes.
   135	  if (io.fs.existsSync(`${p.dir}/purge.marker.json`)) {
   136	    warnings.push('an interrupted purge left its marker — run baton purge-transcript to finish scrubbing every retained copy');
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  let bundle = resolveBase(io, p, warnings);
   140	  if (bundle === null) return { bundle: null, warnings };
   141	
   142	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   143	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
   144	    for (const w of jw) {
   145	      warnings.push(`journal line ${w.line}: ${w.kind}${w.kind === 'seq-order' ? ` (prev ${w.prev}, got ${w.seq})` : ''}`);
   146	    }
   147	    for (const e of entries) {
   148	      // Envelope guard (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable but non-object line
   149	      // (null, number, bare string) is not an event — skip it with a warning
   150	      // rather than crash replay on `e.seq` / `e.dedupeKey`.
   151	      if (e === null || typeof e !== 'object' || Array.isArray(e)) {
   152	        warnings.push('skipped a malformed journal line (not an event envelope)');
   153	        continue;
   154	      }
   155	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq <= bundle.journalSeq) continue;
   156	      bundle = applyEvent(bundle, e);
   157	    }
   158	  }
   159	  return { bundle, warnings };
   160	}
   161	
   162	/**
   163	 * Lock-context snapshot write: caller already holds the repo lock and passes
   164	 * its fencing token; the write is fence-checked immediately before landing
   165	 * (the fast-abort layer — gate-2 fix, reviewer-b finding 12).
   166	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io @param {string} token
   167	 */
   168	export function writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token) {
   169	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   170	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   171	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   172	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   173	    // Size discipline runs in the write path itself (gate-2 fix 10): every
   174	    // persisted snapshot is within budget; identity for in-budget bundles.
   175	    const bounded = compact(bundle);
   176	    // Validate-before-backup (gate-2 major 15): a corrupt current snapshot
   177	    // must never overwrite a good .bak — the backup only rotates when the
   178	    // outgoing snapshot itself would be recoverable.
   179	    const prev = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   180	    if (prev.ok && validateBundle(prev.value).ok) {
   181	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.bak, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
   182	    }
   183	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.snapshot, JSON.stringify(bounded, null, 2) + '\n');
   184	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.handoffMd, renderHandoffMd(bounded));
   185	  });
   186	}
   187	
   188	/**
   189	 * Persist a snapshot: back up the previous good one, write atomically, render
   190	 * HANDOFF.md alongside. Mutation — runs under the repo lock.
   191	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {any} io
   192	 */
   193	export function writeSnapshot(root, bundle, io) {
   194	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => writeSnapshotIn(root, bundle, io, token));
   195	}
   196	
   197	/**
   198	 * Append one journal entry under the repo lock. A pre-numbered entry keeps its
   199	 * seq; a seq-less entry is allocated max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) + 1.
   200	 * @param {string} root @param {any} entry @param {any} io
   201	 * @returns {number} the entry's (possibly allocated) seq
   202	 */
   203	export function appendJournal(root, entry, io) {
   204	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   205	  return withLock(root, io, (token) =>
   206	    guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   207	      assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   208	      ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   209	      let seq = entry.seq;
   210	      let toAppend = entry;
   211	      if (typeof seq !== 'number') {
   212	        const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   213	        const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   214	        let tailSeq = 0;
   215	        if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   216	          for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
   217	            if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
   218	          }
   219	        }
   220	        seq = Math.max(snapSeq, tailSeq) + 1;
   221	        toAppend = { ...entry, seq };
   222	      }
   223	      appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, toAppend);
   224	      return seq;
   225	    }),
   226	  );
   227	}
   228	
   229	/**
   230	 * Crash-safe journal rotation (marker → freeze → rename → fresh journal →
   231	 * clear marker), with 10-per-kind history retention. Mutation — lock-gated.
   232	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io
   233	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
   234	 */
   235	export function rotateJournal(root, kind, io) {
   236	  return withLock(root, io, (token) => rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token));
   237	}
   238	
   239	/**
   240	 * Lock-context rotation: caller holds the lock and passes its fencing token.
   241	 * @param {string} root @param {'finalize'|'takeover'|'receive'} kind @param {any} io @param {string} token
   242	 * @returns {string} the history stem for this rotation
   243	 */
   244	export function rotateJournalIn(root, kind, io, token) {
   245	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   246	  return guardedWrite(root, io, token, () => {
   247	    assertHandoffTreeSafe(root, io);
   248	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   249	    const startedAt = io.now();
   250	    const stem = `${tsForFile(startedAt)}.${kind}`;
   251	    const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, p.snapshot);
   252	    const seq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   253	
   254	    atomicWriteJson(io.fs, markerPath(p), { kind, startedAt, seq });
   255	    ensureDir(io.fs, p.historyDir);
   256	
   257	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.snapshot)) {
   258	      // Atomic freeze (gate-2 minor 16): tmp+rename, so a crash mid-copy can
   259	      // never leave a partial history snapshot that recovery would trust.
   260	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, io.fs.readFileSync(p.snapshot, 'utf8'));
   261	    } else {
   262	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.json`, '');
   263	    }
   264	    if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   265	      io.fs.renameSync(p.journal, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`);
   266	    } else {
   267	      atomicWriteText(io.fs, `${p.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`, '');
   268	    }
   269	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, p.journal, '');
   270	
   271	    // Retention: newest RETAIN_PER_KIND per kind (ISO-derived stems sort by time).
   272	    const freezes = io.fs
   273	      .readdirSync(p.historyDir)
   274	      .filter((/** @type {string} */ n) => n.endsWith(`.${kind}.json`))
   275	      .sort();
   276	    while (freezes.length > RETAIN_PER_KIND) {
   277	      const oldest = /** @type {string} */ (freezes.shift());
   278	      const oldStem = oldest.slice(0, -'.json'.length);
   279	      for (const suffix of ['.json', '.ndjson']) {
   280	        try {
   281	          io.fs.unlinkSync(`${p.historyDir}/${oldStem}${suffix}`);
   282	        } catch {
   283	          // already absent — retention is best-effort per file
   284	        }
   285	      }
   286	    }
   287	
   288	    io.fs.unlinkSync(markerPath(p));
   289	    return stem;
   290	  });
   291	}

     3	/**
     4	 * The --json contract: exactly one compact envelope line on stdout, nothing else.
     5	 * @param {any} io
     6	 * @param {{ok: boolean, data?: any, warnings?: any[], error?: {code: string, msg: string} | null}} env

     8	export function emitEnvelope(io, env) {
     9	  const full = { ok: env.ok, data: env.data ?? null, warnings: env.warnings ?? [], error: env.error ?? null };
    10	  io.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(full) + '\n');

    13	/**
    14	 * Centralized usage-error emission (gate-2 fix 11): with --json the envelope
    15	 * contract holds even for bad invocations; otherwise a one-line stderr
    16	 * message. Always returns the usage exit code 2.
    17	 * @param {any} io @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags

    21	export function usageError(io, flags, cmd, msg) {
    22	  if (flags.json === true) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'usage', msg } });
    23	  else io.stderr.write(`baton ${cmd}: ${msg}\n`);
    24	  return 2;

    26	
    27	/**
    28	 * Repo-root discovery (plan §Root discovery): `--root` wins; else the nearest
    29	 * ancestor of io.cwd carrying `.handoff/` or `baton.config.json`; else the
    30	 * nearest ancestor carrying `.git` (the repo toplevel — and a boundary the
    31	 * walk never crosses, so a nested repo resolves to itself); else io.cwd.
    32	 * @param {any} io @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
    33	 * @returns {string}
    34	 */
    35	export function resolveRoot(io, flags) {
    36	  if (typeof flags.root === 'string' && flags.root.length > 0) return flags.root;
    37	  // Normalize separators up front (gate-2 iter-2 M2): a Windows cwd arrives
    38	  // backslash-separated, but Node fs accepts forward slashes, so the ascent
    39	  // and the marker checks work in one forward-slash space. The last separator
    40	  // is either kind; the loop stops at a drive/UNC or filesystem root.
    41	  const start = normSep(io.cwd);
    42	  let dir = start;
    43	  while (true) {
    44	    try {
    45	      if (io.fs.existsSync(joinNorm(dir, '.handoff')) || io.fs.existsSync(joinNorm(dir, 'baton.config.json'))) return dir;
    46	      if (io.fs.existsSync(joinNorm(dir, '.git'))) return dir; // repo toplevel; never walk past a .git boundary
    47	    } catch {
    48	      break;
    49	    }
    50	    const cut = dir.lastIndexOf('/');
    51	    if (cut < 0) break;
    52	    const parent = cut === 0 ? '/' : dir.slice(0, cut);
    53	    // At a drive-relative ceiling ("C:") examine the drive ROOT ("C:/") once —
    54	    // a repo can live directly at C:/ (iter-3 F6) — then stop.
    55	    if (/^[A-Za-z]:$/.test(parent)) {
    56	      const driveRoot = `${parent}/`;
    57	      if (driveRoot !== dir) {
    58	        dir = driveRoot;
    59	        continue;
    60	      }
    61	      break;
    62	    }
    63	    if (parent === dir) break;
    64	    dir = parent;
    65	  }
    66	  return start;
    67	}

    69	/**
    70	 * True when this process is a loop-supervised child (BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD
    71	 * set to any non-empty value). Supervised children must never read or write
    72	 * any bundle and never receive resume context — hook-invoked commands check

    74	 * supervisor never has to filter child output or nonzero exits.
    75	 * @param {any} io

    78	export function isSupervisedChild(io) {
    79	  const v = io?.env?.BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD;

    83	/** The supported platform enum, validated wherever a platform id is intake. */
    84	export const PLATFORMS = ['claude-code', 'codex', 'cursor'];
    85	
    86	/**
    87	 * Validate a platform id against the enum (surface-audit fold): a typo'd
    88	 * platform used to silently seed persistent state whose bogus origin then
    89	 * rejected the REAL platform's checkpoints as foreign.
    90	 * @param {string | boolean | undefined} value
    91	 * @returns {string | null} an error message, or null when valid/absent
    92	 */
    93	export function platformError(value) {
    94	  if (value === undefined) return null;
    95	  if (typeof value === 'string' && PLATFORMS.includes(value)) return null;
    96	  return `platform must be one of ${PLATFORMS.join('|')} (got ${JSON.stringify(value)})`;
    97	}
    98	
    99	/**
   100	 * Strict flag parser (surface-audit fold; docs/design/core.md §tooling always
   101	 * specified strict parsing). spec maps each flag name to 'string' | 'boolean';
   102	 * `json` and `root` are implied common flags. Errors — unknown flag, stray
   103	 * positional, missing string-flag value — return {error} so the command exits
   104	 * 2 instead of silently losing intent: the lenient parser let a stray token
   105	 * flip SAFETY flags off (checkpoint/doctor --strict, remap --native-only,
   106	 * receive --print-prompt all read fail-dangerous exit 0).
   107	 * @param {string[]} args
   108	 * @param {Record<string, 'string' | 'boolean'>} spec
   109	 * @returns {{flags: Record<string, string | boolean>, error?: string}}
   110	 */
   111	export function parseFlagsStrict(args, spec) {
   112	  /** @type {Record<string, 'string' | 'boolean'>} */
   113	  const full = { json: 'boolean', root: 'string', ...spec };
   114	  /** @type {Record<string, string | boolean>} */
   115	  const flags = {};
   116	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
   117	    const a = args[i];
   118	    if (!a.startsWith('--')) return { flags, error: `unexpected argument '${a}' — flags are --name [value]` };
   119	    const key = a.slice(2);
   120	    const kind = full[key];
   121	    if (kind === undefined) return { flags, error: `unknown flag --${key}` };
   122	    if (kind === 'boolean') {
   123	      // A following token that is not a flag would have been this flag's
   124	      // silent "value" under the lenient parser; reject it loudly.
   125	      const next = args[i + 1];
   126	      if (next !== undefined && !next.startsWith('--')) {
   127	        return { flags, error: `unexpected token '${next}' after --${key} (a bare flag takes no value)` };
   128	      }
   129	      flags[key] = true;
   130	      continue;
   131	    }
   132	    const next = args[i + 1];
   133	    // A string flag consumes the next token unless it is itself a KNOWN flag
   134	    // (so `--commit --origin x` errors instead of eating '--origin').
   135	    if (next === undefined || (next.startsWith('--') && full[next.slice(2)] !== undefined)) {
   136	      return { flags, error: `--${key} requires a value` };
   137	    }
   138	    flags[key] = next;
   139	    i += 1;
   140	  }
   141	  return { flags };
   142	}
   143	
   144	/**
   145	 * Minimal flag parser: `--key value` pairs (value = next token not starting
   146	 * with --), bare `--key` booleans, positionals collected in order.
   147	 * @param {string[]} args
   148	 * @returns {{flags: Record<string, string | boolean>, positionals: string[]}}
   149	 */
   150	export function parseFlags(args) {
   151	  /** @type {Record<string, string | boolean>} */
   152	  const flags = {};
   153	  /** @type {string[]} */
   154	  const positionals = [];
   155	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
   156	    const a = args[i];
   157	    if (a.startsWith('--')) {
   158	      const key = a.slice(2);
   159	      const next = args[i + 1];
   160	      if (next !== undefined && !next.startsWith('--')) {
   161	        flags[key] = next;
   162	        i += 1;
   163	      } else {
   164	        flags[key] = true;
   165	      }
   166	    } else {
   167	      positionals.push(a);
   168	    }
   169	  }
   170	  return { flags, positionals };
   171	}

     1	import { jailRelPath } from '../util/jail.mjs';

     3	const RING_CAP = 500;
     4	
     5	/** Audit note for a refused payload — visible in the decision log, never a throw. */
     6	const refusalNote = (/** @type {string} */ type, /** @type {any} */ detail) =>
     7	  `[${type} with invalid payload refused: ${JSON.stringify(detail).slice(0, 120)}]`;
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * Pure event reducer. Returns a new bundle; never mutates the input.
    11	 * Idempotent: an event whose dedupeKey is still in the bundle's ring is a no-op.
    12	 * Unknown event types degrade to a decision-log note — a checkpoint must never
    13	 * fail because a newer adapter emitted something this core doesn't know — and
    14	 * journal payloads are untrusted (gate-2 fix 6): malformed shapes degrade to a
    15	 * refusal note and repository paths are jailed before storage.
    16	 * @param {any} bundle
    17	 * @param {{seq: number, ts: string, type: string, dedupeKey: string, writerId?: string, source?: string, payload: any}} event
    18	 */
    19	export function applyEvent(bundle, event) {
    20	  // Journal lines are untrusted (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable null/number/
    21	  // string is a valid JSON line but not an event envelope — degrade to a
    22	  // no-op rather than crash replay. The store's load loop also pre-filters and
    23	  // warns; this is the last-line guard for any direct caller.
    24	  if (event === null || typeof event !== 'object' || Array.isArray(event)) return bundle;
    25	  if (bundle.dedupeRing.includes(event.dedupeKey)) return bundle;
    26	
    27	  const out = structuredClone(bundle);
    28	  const { seq, ts, type, payload = {} } = structuredClone(event);
    29	
    30	  switch (type) {
    31	    case 'decision': {
    32	      /** @type {{seq: number, ts: string, summary: any, detail?: any}} */
    33	      const entry = { seq, ts, summary: payload.summary };
    34	      if (payload.detail !== undefined) entry.detail = payload.detail;
    35	      out.decisions.push(entry);
    36	      break;
    37	    }
    38	    case 'note':
    39	      out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: payload.text });
    40	      // A structured StopFailure rate_limit note marks the open bundle
    41	      // limit-hit (gate-2 fix 3): the unsealed limit death must be visible to
    42	      // the next SessionStart on any platform, not buried in the decision log.
    43	      if (payload.structured?.kind === 'stop-failure' && payload.errorType === 'rate_limit' && out.handoff.status === 'open') {
    44	        out.handoff = { ...out.handoff, reason: out.handoff.reason ?? payload.text ?? null, reasonClass: 'usage-limit' };
    45	      }
    46	      break;
    47	    case 'plan.set':
    48	      if (Array.isArray(payload.steps) && payload.steps.every((/** @type {any} */ s) => s !== null && typeof s === 'object')) {
    49	        out.plan.steps = payload.steps;
    50	      } else {
    51	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('plan.set', payload?.steps) });
    52	      }
    53	      break;
    54	    case 'plan.step': {
    55	      if (payload === null || typeof payload !== 'object' || typeof payload.id !== 'string') {
    56	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('plan.step', payload) });
    57	        break;
    58	      }
    59	      const existing = out.plan.steps.find((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.id === payload.id);
    60	      if (existing) Object.assign(existing, payload);
    61	      else out.plan.steps.push(payload);
    62	      break;
    63	    }
    64	    case 'file.touch': {
    65	      const safePath = jailRelPath(payload?.path);
    66	      if (safePath === null) {
    67	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('file.touch', payload?.path) });
    68	        break;
    69	      }
    70	      const entry = { path: safePath, op: typeof payload.op === 'string' ? payload.op : 'touch', lastTs: ts };
    71	      const i = out.files.touched.findIndex((/** @type {any} */ f) => f.path === safePath);
    72	      if (i === -1) out.files.touched.push(entry);
    73	      else out.files.touched[i] = entry;
    74	      break;
    75	    }
    76	    case 'roles.remap': {
    77	      // The assignments object AND every value must be non-null objects (iter-3
    78	      // F3): a well-formed {planner: null} used to be stored verbatim and then
    79	      // crashed renderHandoffMd. Untrusted journal input degrades to a note.
    80	      const a = payload?.assignments;
    81	      const wellFormed =
    82	        a !== null &&
    83	        typeof a === 'object' &&
    84	        !Array.isArray(a) &&
    85	        Object.values(a).every((/** @type {any} */ v) => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v));
    86	      if (wellFormed) {
    87	        out.roles.assignments = a;
    88	      } else {
    89	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('roles.remap', a) });
    90	      }
    91	      break;
    92	    }
    93	    case 'task.update': {
    94	      /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    95	      const patch = {};
    96	      if (typeof payload?.goal === 'string') patch.goal = payload.goal;
    97	      if (Array.isArray(payload?.constraints) && payload.constraints.every((/** @type {any} */ c) => typeof c === 'string')) {
    98	        patch.constraints = payload.constraints;
    99	      }
   100	      if (Array.isArray(payload?.acceptance)) patch.acceptance = payload.acceptance;
   101	      if (Object.keys(patch).length === 0) {
   102	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('task.update', payload) });
   103	        break;
   104	      }
   105	      out.task = { ...out.task, ...patch };
   106	      break;
   107	    }
   108	    case 'git.update':
   109	      if (payload === null || typeof payload === 'object') out.git = payload;
   110	      else out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('git.update', payload) });
   111	      break;
   112	    case 'bundle.seed':
   113	      // Self-applying identity (gate-2 iter-2 B6): the auto-seed emits this as
   114	      // the journal's first event so a journal-only rebuild (snapshot + .bak
   115	      // both gone) restores the bundle's identity/origin/task instead of the
   116	      // "unknown" placeholder resolveBase seeds. Replayed first (lowest seq),
   117	      // so later events layer on top; on normal replay its seq is below the
   118	      // snapshot's journalSeq and it is skipped.
   119	      if (payload && typeof payload === 'object') {
   120	        if (typeof payload.bundleId === 'string') out.bundleId = payload.bundleId;
   121	        if (typeof payload.generation === 'number') out.generation = payload.generation;
   122	        if (typeof payload.createdAt === 'string') out.createdAt = payload.createdAt;
   123	        // Restore identity ONLY (platform/model) — never sessionHint/unstable.
   124	        // Session ownership is adopted AFTER the seed (a snapshot-only mutation),
   125	        // so replaying the seed must not clobber a later-adopted hint back to
   126	        // its seed-time null (gate-2 iter-2: caught by the adoption test).
   127	        if (payload.origin && typeof payload.origin === 'object') {
   128	          if (typeof payload.origin.platform === 'string') out.origin.platform = payload.origin.platform;
   129	          if (typeof payload.origin.model === 'string') out.origin.model = payload.origin.model;
   130	        }
   131	        if (payload.task && typeof payload.task === 'object' && typeof payload.task.goal === 'string') {
   132	          out.task.goal = payload.task.goal;
   133	        }
   134	      }
   135	      break;
   136	    case 'transcript.set':
   137	      // Opt-in transcript tail (plan §Transcript policy): stored under the
   138	      // literal property name `transcript` so purge-transcript strips it from
   139	      // every retained copy. Render never includes it.
   140	      if (payload?.transcript !== null && typeof payload?.transcript === 'object' && typeof payload.transcript.tail === 'string') {
   141	        out.transcript = {
   142	          capturedAt: typeof payload.transcript.capturedAt === 'string' ? payload.transcript.capturedAt : ts,
   143	          tail: payload.transcript.tail,
   144	        };
   145	      } else {
   146	        out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: refusalNote('transcript.set', 'malformed payload') });
   147	      }
   148	      break;
   149	    default:
   150	      out.decisions.push({ seq, ts, summary: `[unhandled event type '${type}' recorded as note]` });
   151	      break;
   152	  }
   153	
   154	  out.updatedAt = ts;
   155	  // Harden against foreign/keyless entries (gate-2 fix): a missing seq must
   156	  // never poison journalSeq to NaN, and an absent dedupeKey must never enter
   157	  // the ring (undefined in the ring dedupes every later keyless event).
   158	  if (typeof seq === 'number' && Number.isFinite(seq)) out.journalSeq = Math.max(out.journalSeq, seq);
   159	  if (typeof event.dedupeKey === 'string' && event.dedupeKey.length > 0) {
   160	    out.dedupeRing.push(event.dedupeKey);
   161	    if (out.dedupeRing.length > RING_CAP) out.dedupeRing.splice(0, out.dedupeRing.length - RING_CAP);
   162	  }
   163	
   164	  return out;
   165	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { makeIo } from '../helpers/fakeio.mjs';
     4	import { cmdCheckpoint } from '../../core/src/commands/checkpoint.mjs';
     5	import { bundlePaths } from '../../core/src/bundle/store.mjs';
     6	import { readAllTolerant } from '../../core/src/util/jsonl.mjs';
     7	
     8	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     9	// Command-level contract for core/src/commands/checkpoint.mjs. Called by DIRECT
    10	// import over fakeio, mirroring cmdDetect/cmdRemap: cmdCheckpoint(args, io) ->
    11	// exit code (this file `await`s it, so it is agnostic to sync/async; a checkpoint
    12	// that refreshes git is async). stdin arrives as the STRING io.stdin.
    13	//
    14	// Source of truth: docs/design/core.md §CLI contract ("Checkpoint soft-fails to
    15	// exit 0 in hook contexts (--strict opts out)"); plan §Checkpoint engine
    16	// ("adapters send pre-normalized baton/event@1 on stdin … journal append always,
    17	// snapshot rewrite throttled (>=10 events / 30 s / important event types) …
    18	// Hook-safety rule: exit 0 on all soft failures"); §Concurrency ("a checkpoint
    19	// whose sessionHint/origin doesn't match the active bundle is rejected with an
    20	// actionable warning; --take-over supersedes: archives the current bundle to
    21	// history/ and starts fresh"); §normalize (checkpoint runs stdin through
    22	// normalizeHookPayload — baton/event@1 passes through verbatim).
    23	//
    24	// PINS:
    25	//   1. INPUT: stdin is a JSON payload; checkpoint parses it and runs it through
    26	//      normalize (baton/event@1 -> passthrough). Each normalized event is stamped
    27	//      (ts=io.now(), a non-empty string dedupeKey, an allocated numeric seq) and
    28	//      appended to the journal — the stamps are asserted on the appended entry
    29	//      (verifier fold F6: dedupe idempotency needs the key actually persisted).
    30	//   2. IMPORTANT vs ROUTINE (throttle). Journal append ALWAYS. A snapshot rewrite
    31	//      (bundle.json + HANDOFF.md re-rendered) happens when the event type is
    32	//      IMPORTANT, OR >30s elapsed since the snapshot's updatedAt, OR >=10 events
    33	//      have accreted since the last snapshot write (all three triggers tested;
    34	//      F6 added the count trigger). A 'note' is ROUTINE; a 'decision' is
    35	//      IMPORTANT. When throttled, the journal grows but bundle.json is
    36	//      byte-unchanged.
    37	//   3. MISSING BUNDLE: no .handoff -> checkpoint SEEDS a fresh bundle, applies the
    38	//      event, and warns; exit 0.
    39	//   4. HOOK SAFETY: unparseable stdin -> exit 0 + a warning; --strict -> exit 1.
    40	//   5. FOREIGN SESSION (semantic isolation, plan §Concurrency):
    41	//      (a) an incoming STABLE sessionHint that differs from the active bundle's
    42	//          stable origin.sessionHint is REJECTED — exit STILL 0 (hook safety), a
    43	//          warning NAMES --take-over, and the ENTIRE memfs is byte-identical
    44	//          (F4: nothing may land in journal.ndjson either — a journaled foreign
    45	//          event would replay into the bundle later).
    46	//      (b) an origin PLATFORM mismatch (--platform differs from the bundle's
    47	//          origin.platform, stable hint matching) is rejected the same way (F5 —
    48	//          the plan gates on sessionHint/ORIGIN).
    49	//      (c) an UNSTABLE incoming hint NEVER triggers rejection on its own (plan
    50	//          §sessionHint derivation; F5): the event is applied normally, with a
    51	//          warning only.
    52	//   6. --take-over: archives the active bundle via rotateJournal kind 'takeover'
    53	//      and starts a FRESH bundle owned by the incoming session (origin.platform =
    54	//      --platform, origin.sessionHint = incoming hint), then applies the event.
    55	//
    56	// Session facts ride on the (passthrough) event's sessionHint/unstable fields.
    57	// Seeds whose origin.sessionHint is null never trigger the foreign check (nothing
    58	// stable to compare), keeping the throttle/seed tests free of session noise.
    59	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60	
    61	const T0 = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z';
    62	const paths = bundlePaths('/repo');
    63	
    64	function mkBundle(overrides = {}) {
    65	  return {
    66	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
    67	    bundleId: 'b_ckpt0000000000',
    68	    generation: 1,
    69	    createdAt: T0,
    70	    updatedAt: T0,
    71	    origin: { platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5', sessionHint: null, unstable: false },
    72	    task: { goal: 'Build the checkpoint cmd', constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
    73	    plan: { steps: [] },
    74	    decisions: [],
    75	    files: { touched: [] },
    76	    roles: { assignments: {} },
    77	    git: null,
    78	    handoff: { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
    79	    journalSeq: 0,
    80	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
    81	    dedupeRing: [],
    82	    ...overrides,
    83	  };
    84	}

   267	    assert.ok(!readSnapshot(io).decisions.some((d) => d.summary === 'from-codex'));
   268	  });

   272	    // rejection on their own, only a warning".
   273	    const seed = mkBundle({

   276	    const io = makeIo({

   288	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), /session|unstable/i, 'a warning is still surfaced');
   289	  });
   290	
   291	  it('--take-over archives the active bundle (rotateJournal takeover) and starts a fresh bundle for the new session', async () => {
   292	    const seed = mkBundle({
   293	      origin: { platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5', sessionHint: 'sess-A', unstable: false },
   294	      decisions: [{ seq: 1, ts: T0, summary: 'OLD-DECISION' }],
   295	      journalSeq: 1,
   296	    });
   297	    const io = makeIo({
   298	      files: {
   299	        [paths.snapshot]: snapText(seed),
   300	        [paths.journal]: JSON.stringify({ seq: 1, ts: T0, type: 'decision', dedupeKey: 'k1', payload: { summary: 'OLD-DECISION' } }) + '\n',
   301	      },
   302	      stdin: event({ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'from-session-B' }, sessionHint: 'sess-B', unstable: false }),
   303	    });
   304	
   305	    const code = await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'codex', '--take-over'], io);
   306	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   307	
   308	    const historyNames = io.fs.readdirSync(paths.historyDir);
   309	    assert.ok(historyNames.some((n) => /\.takeover\.json$/.test(n)), 'the prior bundle is archived as a takeover rotation');
   310	
   311	    const snap = readSnapshot(io);
   312	    assert.equal(snap.origin.sessionHint, 'sess-B', 'the fresh bundle is owned by the taking-over session');
   313	    assert.equal(snap.origin.platform, 'codex', 'the fresh bundle adopts the taking-over platform');
   314	    assert.ok(snap.decisions.some((d) => d.summary === 'from-session-B'), 'the new event applies to the fresh bundle');
   315	    assert.ok(!snap.decisions.some((d) => d.summary === 'OLD-DECISION'), 'the fresh bundle does NOT inherit the archived session\'s decisions');
   316	  });
   317	});
   318	
   319	describe('checkpoint — tolerant stdin shapes (live /baton:handoff failure)', () => {
   320	  // Field finding: the handoff command says "send baton/event@1 events on
   321	  // stdin" and a live model sent one event PER LINE (NDJSON). The old parser
   322	  // rejected everything but a single JSON value, so the narrative checkpoint
   323	  // silently failed. Accept the shapes a model plausibly produces: a single
   324	  // {schema, events} object (canonical), a bare array of events, and NDJSON
   325	  // where every line is an event object — anything else stays bad-stdin.
   326	  it('NDJSON stdin (one event per line) journals every line', async () => {
   327	    const io = makeIo({
   328	      files: { [paths.snapshot]: snapText(mkBundle()) },
   329	      stdin:
   330	        JSON.stringify({ schema: 'baton/event@1', type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'ndjson-line-1' } }) +
   331	        '\n' +
   332	        JSON.stringify({ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'ndjson-line-2' } }) +
   333	        '\n',
   334	    });
   335	    const code = await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io);
   336	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   337	    const sums = journalEntries(io).map((e) => e.payload?.summary);
   338	    assert.ok(sums.includes('ndjson-line-1') && sums.includes('ndjson-line-2'), `both NDJSON events journaled; got ${sums}`);
   339	  });
   340	
   341	  it('a bare JSON array of events journals every element', async () => {
   342	    const io = makeIo({
   343	      files: { [paths.snapshot]: snapText(mkBundle()) },
   344	      stdin: JSON.stringify([
   345	        { type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'arr-1' } },
   346	        { type: 'note', payload: { text: 'arr-2' } },
   347	      ]),
   348	    });
   349	    const code = await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io);
   350	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   351	    const entries = journalEntries(io);
   352	    assert.equal(entries.length, 2, 'both array elements journaled');
   353	  });
   354	
   355	  it('NDJSON with a non-event line is still bad-stdin (no partial application)', async () => {
   356	    const io = makeIo({
   357	      files: { [paths.snapshot]: snapText(mkBundle()) },
   358	      stdin: JSON.stringify({ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'good' } }) + '\nnot json at all\n',
   359	    });
   360	    const code = await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code', '--strict'], io);
   361	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'strict mode hard-fails');
   362	    assert.equal(journalEntries(io).length, 0, 'nothing was journaled from the torn input');
   363	  });
   364	
   365	  it('an array with a non-event element is bad-stdin, not a partial apply', async () => {
   366	    const io = makeIo({
   367	      files: { [paths.snapshot]: snapText(mkBundle()) },
   368	      stdin: JSON.stringify([{ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'good' } }, 'garbage-string']),
   369	    });
   370	    const code = await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code', '--strict'], io);
   371	    assert.equal(code, 1);
   372	    assert.equal(journalEntries(io).length, 0);
   373	  });
   374	});
   375	
   376	describe('checkpoint — schema-miss and bare-event stdin (audit findings 2/13/17)', () => {
   377	  // A close-miss of the documented wrapper must ERROR, not silently degrade to
   378	  // a junk "hook unknown" note with ok:true — a model cannot self-correct from
   379	  // a success envelope while its narrative was discarded.
   380	  it('an events array with the schema key MISSING is bad-stdin, not a silent junk note', async () => {
   381	    const io = makeIo({
   382	      files: { [paths.snapshot]: snapText(mkBundle()) },
   383	      stdin: JSON.stringify({ events: [{ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'lost-narrative' } }] }),
   384	    });
   385	    const code = await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code', '--strict'], io);
   386	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'strict mode hard-fails');
   387	    assert.equal(journalEntries(io).length, 0, 'nothing journaled');
   388	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), /schema/i, 'the error names the schema field so the model can self-correct');
   389	  });
   390	
   391	  it('an events array with a WRONG schema string is bad-stdin too', async () => {
   392	    const io = makeIo({
   393	      files: { [paths.snapshot]: snapText(mkBundle()) },
   394	      stdin: JSON.stringify({ schema: 'baton/events@1', events: [{ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'x' } }] }),
   395	    });
   396	    const code = await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code', '--strict'], io);
   397	    assert.equal(code, 1);
   398	    assert.equal(journalEntries(io).length, 0);
   399	  });
   400	
   401	  it('a single bare event object (no schema, not a hook payload) is accepted as that event', async () => {
   402	    const io = makeIo({
   403	      files: { [paths.snapshot]: snapText(mkBundle()) },
   404	      stdin: JSON.stringify({ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'bare-event' } }),
   405	    });
   406	    const code = await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io);
   407	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   408	    const entries = journalEntries(io);
   409	    assert.equal(entries.length, 1);
   410	    assert.equal(entries[0].type, 'decision');
   411	    assert.equal(entries[0].payload.summary, 'bare-event');
   412	  });
   413	
   414	  it('a hook payload carrying a `type` field is NOT mistaken for a bare event', async () => {
   415	    // hook_event_name marks it a raw harness payload; the extractor tier owns it.
   416	    const io = makeIo({
   417	      files: { [paths.snapshot]: snapText(mkBundle()) },
   418	      stdin: JSON.stringify({ hook_event_name: 'Stop', session_id: 's9', type: 'weird-harness-field' }),
   419	    });
   420	    await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io);
   421	    const entries = journalEntries(io);
   422	    assert.equal(entries[0].type, 'note', 'extractor tier handles it (a Stop note), not the bare-event tier');
   423	    assert.equal(entries[0].payload.trigger, 'Stop');
   424	  });
   425	});
   426	
   427	describe('checkpoint — hint-less narrative cannot merge into a FOREIGN platform bundle (audit finding 1)', () => {
   428	  const codexOwned = () =>
   429	    mkBundle({ origin: { platform: 'codex', model: 'gpt-5.6-sol', sessionHint: 'codex-sess-1', unstable: false } });

   437	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'hook-safety: soft exit');
   438	    assert.equal(journalEntries(io).length, 0, 'the foreign narrative must not merge into the codex-owned bundle');

   446	    });

   448	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   449	    assert.equal(journalEntries(io).length, 1, 'same-platform hint-less narrative applies');
   450	  });

   456	    });

   458	    assert.equal(code, 0);

   461	  });

   465	  it('a raw cursor stop payload (no event field) + --trigger stop journals trigger=stop', async () => {
   466	    // Cursor's stop payload names the event nowhere normalize can find it, so

   471	      stdin: JSON.stringify({ conversation_id: 'c1', workspace_roots: ['/repo'] }),
   472	    });

   475	    assert.equal(code, 0);

   480	    assert.equal(note.source, 'cursor');
   481	  });
   482	
   483	  it('without --trigger the same payload still degrades to unknown (guards the regression the flag fixes)', async () => {

   486	      stdin: JSON.stringify({ conversation_id: 'c1' }),
   487	    });
   488	    await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'cursor'], io);
   489	    const note = journalEntries(io).find((e) => e.type === 'note');
   490	    assert.equal(note.payload.trigger, 'unknown');
   491	  });
